LowpassT

LowpassT calculates proportional element with first order time constant.

| Hint |
| --- |
| T cannot be negative. T should be greater than or equal to 1. |

 

Example Implementation:

Methods:

| Method | Description |
| --- | --- |
| reset | Initialization of the internal filter value(memory). |
| compute | Computes the new low pass  filter value using the filter |
| out | Returns the new lowpass value |

Parameter:

| Parameter | Description | Implementation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| in | input value | R32, R32U32 :real32 OneToOne   S16:sint16  S8:sint8  U16:uint16  U8:uint8  KS16:sint16 KS8:sint8 KU16:uint16 KU8:uint8 compu : user defined |
| initValue | Initialization Value | R32, R32U32 :real32  OneToOne   S16:sint16  S8:sint8  U16:uint16  U8:uint8  KS16:sint16 KS8:sint8 KU16:uint16 KU8:uint8 compu : user defined |
| T | gain constant | R32:uint16 R32U32:uint32 conv\_x   S16:uint16  S8:uint8  KS16:uint16 KS8:uint8 U16:uint16 U8:uint8  KU16:uint16 compu : user defined |
| memory | Stores the lowpass Value | R32, R32U32 :real32  OneToOne   S16:sint32  S8:sint16  KS16:sint32 KS8:sint16 U16:uint32 U8:uint16  KU16:uint32 KU8:uint16  compu : user defined |
| return | new lowpass value | R32, R32U32 :real32  OneToOne   S16:sint16  S8:sint8  U16:uint16  U8:uint8  KS16:sint16 KS8:sint8 KU16:uint16 KU8:uint8 compu : user defined |

| --- |
